\begin{abstract}Every move below was produced by relational analogy to named master games (human and/or top-engine -- the exact source is named per move). Each figure shows our position with Melanie's move (a) and the top retrieved analog positions (b--d) with the master move played there; the caption names the exact source and the piece-role correspondence under $\mu$ -- cross-type maps (a rook playing a queen's role) are called out, as no material/eval engine explains a move that way.\end{abstract}

\caption{\textbf{Move 7, Black: \texttt{O-O}.} (a) Our position; Melanie plays \texttt{O-O} (\texttt{e8g8}). Panels (b)--(d) are the retrieved master positions whose correspondence most drove this move (highest QAP objective among those that transfer to it). (b) Retrieved from \texttt{lichess\_elite\_2024-09\_p0022\#588} (ply 23) [structural distance $d$=83, QAP obj=51]: the master played \texttt{O-O}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Ke1} plays the role of that game's \emph{Ke1}; cross-type roles: our \emph{R}a8 as their \emph{P}a6, our \emph{n}c3 as their \emph{b}f3; transferred move \texttt{e8g8} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. (c) Retrieved from \texttt{lichess\_elite\_2024-09\_p0023\#2374} (ply 26) [structural distance $d$=75, QAP obj=40]: the master played \texttt{O-O}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Ke1} plays the role of that game's \emph{Ke1}; cross-type roles: our \emph{P}c7 as their \emph{R}h1, our \emph{P}g7 as their \emph{N}e2; transferred move \texttt{e8g8} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. (d) Retrieved from \texttt{lichess\_elite\_2024-09\_p0021\#3677} (ply 21) [structural distance $d$=88, QAP obj=31]: the master played \texttt{O-O}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Ke1} plays the role of that game's \emph{Ke1}; cross-type roles: our \emph{N}b8 as their \emph{R}a8, our \emph{R}h8 as their \emph{Q}d8; transferred move \texttt{e8g8} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. Synthesis: 3 retrieved analogs transferred to \texttt{O-O} (summed QAP objective 122, the strongest correspondence among the candidate moves); Melanie committed it via the \texttt{search} selector.}
\label{fig:thought-m13}
\end{figure}
